commit 9f90dac1120a56fe7ba93940fe59df42e9bda308

Author: JohnMcPMS <johnmcp@microsoft.com>
Date:   Fri, 28 Apr 2023 11:01:05 -0700

Enable building everything needed in PowerShell with static runtime (#3193)

Adds a solution and project configurations for building everything needed for PowerShell with static runtime linkage.  This enables us to produce modules without any external dependencies (beyond PowerShell of course).
